It is in the file `datapaper.pdf`\n    16→  - A recent paper that uses this dataset is \"Brain-wide analysis reveals movement encoding structured across and within brain areas\". It is in the file `methodpaper.pdf`\n    17→  - A white paper about spike sorting and quality control is in the file `ChenLiuEtAl2023_SpikeSortingQC.pdf`\n    18→- Some parts of the paper that describe the experiment and processing have been copied to the file `methods.txt`. \n    19→- **Code**:\n    20→  - `code` contains code from `methodpaper.pdf`.\n    21→- **Data** from the papers are in the directory `data`.\n    22→\n    23→## Decoder Task\n    24→\n    25→- Temporally align based on **Go cue onset**. \n    26→- Extract **2.5 s before to 1.5 s after** the go cue for each trial\n    27→- Use **50-ms-width bins** for computing firing rates. \n    28→\n    29→### Decoder Inputs:\n    30→- Time from **tone onset** in seconds (continuous, time-varying)\n    31→- Whether **photostimulation** is on at every time point (discrete, time-varying)\n    32→\n    33→### Decoder Outputs\n    34→- Lick direction **choice** (left = 0, right = 1, per-trial)\n    35→- **Outcome** (ignore = 0, miss = 1, hit = 2, per-trial)\n    36→- **Early lick** (no = 0, yes = 1, per-trial)\n    37→- **Tongue y-position**, per-session discretization:\n    38→  - 0: < 40th percentile of y-position over the session\n    39→  - 1: 40th to 60th percentile of y-position over the session\n    40→  - 2: > 60th percentile of y-position over the session\n    41→\n    42→## Target Data Format\n    43→\n    44→- Save the full converted dataset to the pickle file `converted_data.pkl`.\n    45→- All datasets must be converted into the following Python dictionary structure:\n    46→\n    47→```python\n    48→data = {\n    49→    'neural': [  # list of sessions\n    50→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    51→            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n    52→            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\n    53→            ...\n    54→        ],\n    55→        [  # list of trials for session 2\n    56→            ...\n    57→        ],\n    58→        ...\n    59→    ],\n    60→\n    61→    'input': [  # list of sessions\n    62→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    63→            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\n    64→            ...\n    65→        ],\n    66→        ...\n    67→    ],\n    68→\n    69→    'output': [  # list of sessions\n    70→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    71→            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\n    72→            ...\n    73→        ],\n    74→        ...\n    75→    ],\n    76→\n    77→    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\n    78→    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\n    79→\n    80→    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\n    81→    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\n    82→        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\n    83→    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\n    84→\n    85→    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\n    86→    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\n    87→    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\n    88→        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\n    89→        ...\n    90→    ],\n    91→\n    92→    'metadata': {\n    93→        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\n    94→        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\n    95→        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\n    96→        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n    97→        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n    98→        ...\n    99→    }\n   100→}\n   101→```\n   102→\n   103→### Key Format Requirements\n   104→\n   105→- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n   106→  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n   107→  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n   108→  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n   109→\n   110→- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n   111→  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n   112→  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n   113→    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n   114→    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n   115→    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n   116→  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n   117→  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n   118→  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n   119→\n   120→- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n   121→  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n   122→  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n   123→  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n   124→  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n   125→  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n   126→\n   127→- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n   128→- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n   129→  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n   130→\n   131→- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n   132→- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n   133→\n   134→- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n   135→  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n   136→\n   137→- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n   138→  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n   139→- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n   140→  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n   141→\n   142→- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n   143→  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n   144→  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n   145→  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n   146→  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n   147→  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n   148→\n   149→## Decoder Reference\n   150→\n   151→- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n   152→- Optional arguments:\n   153→  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n   154→  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n   155→  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n   156→- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n   157→\n   158→### Purpose\n   159→\n   160→These functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n   161→1. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D7W4JxywRQJKVTLuCeoQTn","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→\"\"\"\n     2→Convert NWB data from the MAP dataset (Li et al.) to the decoder-compatible format.\n     3→\n     4→Reference papers:\n     5→- \"Brain-wide neural activity underlying memory-guided movement\" (data paper)\n     6→- \"Brain-wide analysis reveals movement encoding structured across and within brain areas\" (method paper)\n     7→\n     8→Processing decisions:\n     9→- Align to go cue onset\n    10→- Extract -2.5s to +1.5s relative to go cue\n    11→- 50ms time bins for firing rates (spike counts / bin_width)\n    12→- QC filtering: use 'good' classification from NWB (matches classifier-based QC in paper)\n    13→- Trial filtering: exclude early lick and no-response (ignore) trials\n    14→- Session selection: >65% correct rate on control (no photostim) non-early-lick trials,\n    15→  and at least 50 correct lick-left and lick-right trials each\n    16→- Sessions with 0 good units are skipped\n    17→\"\"\"\n    18→\n    19→import os\n    20→import sys\n    21→import pickle\n    22→import numpy as np\n    23→import pynwb\n    24→from collections import OrderedDict\n    25→import argparse\n    26→import warnings\n    27→warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n    28→\n    29→# ---- Parameters ----\n    30→BIN_WIDTH = 0.05  # 50 ms bins\n    31→BEGIN_TIME = -2.5  # relative to go cue\n    32→END_TIME = 1.5    # relative to go cue\n    33→MIN_PERF = 0.65   # >65% correct\n    34→MIN_CORRECT_PER_SIDE = 50  # at least 50 correct per side\n    35→\n    36→DATA_DIR = '/app/data'\n    37→\n    38→def compute_firing_rates(spike_times_by_neuron, go_cue_time, begin_time, end_time, bin_width):\n    39→    \"\"\"\n    40→    Compute firing rates for a single trial.\n    41→\n    42→    Args:\n    43→        spike_times_by_neuron: list of arrays, spike times for each neuron (absolute times)\n    44→        go_cue_time: float, absolute go cue time for this trial\n    45→        begin_time: float, start of window relative to go cue\n    46→        end_time: float, end of window relative to go cue\n    47→        bin_width: float, bin width in seconds\n    48→\n    49→    Returns:\n    50→        fr: (n_neurons, n_bins) array of firing rates\n    51→        bin_centers: (n_bins,) array of bin centers relative to go cue\n    52→    \"\"\"\n    53→    n_bins = int(round((end_time - begin_time) / bin_width))\n    54→    bin_edges = np.linspace(begin_time, end_time, n_bins + 1) + go_cue_time\n    55→    bin_centers = (bin_edges[:-1] + bin_edges[1:]) / 2.0 - go_cue_time\n    56→\n    57→    n_neurons = len(spike_times_by_neuron)\n    58→    fr = np.zeros((n_neurons, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    59→\n    60→    for i, st in enumerate(spike_times_by_neuron):\n    61→        if len(st) == 0:\n    62→            continue\n    63→        # Only consider spikes in the window\n    64→        mask = (st >= bin_edges[0]) & (st < bin_edges[-1])\n    65→        st_window = st[mask]\n    66→        if len(st_window) > 0:\n    67→            counts, _ = np.histogram(st_window, bins=bin_edges)\n    68→            fr[i, :] = counts.astype(np.float32) / bin_width\n    69→\n    70→    return fr, bin_centers\n    71→\n    72→\n    73→def preload_spike_times(nwb, unit_indices):\n    74→    \"\"\"\n    75→    Preload all spike times for good units into memory.\n    76→    Returns list of arrays, one per unit.\n    77→    \"\"\"\n    78→    return [nwb.units['spike_times'][uid] for uid in unit_indices]\n    79→\n    80→\n    81→def get_spike_times_for_trial_from_cache(all_spike_times, obs_intervals, obs_idx):\n    82→    \"\"\"\n    83→    Get spike times for a specific obs_interval index using preloaded data.\n    84→    \"\"\"\n    85→    spike_times_list = []\n    86→    t_start, t_stop = obs_intervals[obs_idx]\n    87→    for st in all_spike_times:\n    88→        mask = (st >= t_start) & (st < t_stop)\n    89→        spike_times_list.append(st[mask])\n    90→    return spike_times_list\n    91→\n    92→\n    93→def build_obs_to_trial_map(nwb, good_indices):\n    94→    \"\"\"\n    95→    Build mapping from obs_intervals indices to trial table indices.\n    96→    Returns:\n    97→        obs_to_trial: list of trial indices, where obs_to_trial[obs_idx] = trial_idx\n    98→    \"\"\"\n    99→    trials = nwb.trials\n   100→    n_trials = len(trials)\n   101→    units = nwb.units\n   102→\n   103→    # Use the first good unit's obs_intervals (all units have the same)\n   104→    obs = units['obs_intervals'][good_indices[0]]\n   105→    n_obs = obs.shape[0]\n   106→\n   107→    if n_obs == n_trials:\n   108→        # Perfect match - obs[i] corresponds to trial[i]\n   109→        return list(range(n_trials))\n   110→\n   111→    # Match by start time\n   112→    trial_starts = np.array([trials['start_time'][i] for i in range(n_trials)])\n   113→    obs_to_trial = []\n   114→    for i in range(n_obs):\n   115→        diffs = np.abs(trial_starts - obs[i, 0])\n   116→        min_idx = np.argmin(diffs)\n   117→        if diffs[min_idx] < 0.5:  # 0.5s tolerance\n   118→            obs_to_trial.append(int(min_idx))\n   119→        else:\n   120→            obs_to_trial.append(-1)  # no match\n   121→\n   122→    return obs_to_trial\n   123→\n   124→\n   125→def process_session(nwb_path, sample_mode=False):\n   126→    \"\"\"\n   127→    Process one NWB session file.\n   128→\n   129→    Returns None if session doesn't pass criteria, otherwise returns a dict with:\n   130→        - neural: list of (n_neurons, n_bins) arrays per trial\n   131→        - input: list of (n_input, n_bins) arrays per trial\n   132→        - output: list of (n_output,) arrays per trial\n   133→        - brain_region_labels: list of str per neuron\n   134→        - subject_id: str\n   135→        - session_id: str\n   136→        - tongue_y_all: list of tongue_y values for percentile computation\n   137→    \"\"\"\n   138→    io = pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_path, 'r')\n   139→    nwb = io.read()\n   140→\n   141→    trials = nwb.trials\n   142→    n_trials = len(trials)\n   143→    units = nwb.units\n   144→    n_units = len(units)\n   145→\n   146→    subject_id = nwb.subject.description  # Filter trials ----\n   210→    # Per methods: \"Early lick trials and no response trials were excluded for analysis\"\n   211→    # Also must have neural data (be in obs_intervals)\n   212→    valid_trial_indices = []\n   213→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   214→        # Must have neural data\n   215→        if i not in trials_with_neural:\n   216→            continue\n   217→\n   218→        outcome = trials['outcome'][i]\n   219→        early = trials['early_lick'][i]\n   220→\n   221→        # Exclude early lick and ignore (no response) trials\n   222→        if early != 'no early':\n   223→            continue\n   224→        if outcome == 'ignore':\n   225→            continue\n   226→\n   227→        valid_trial_indices.append(i)\n   228→\n   229→    if len(valid_trial_indices) < 2:\n   230→        print(f\"  Skipping {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}: <2 valid trials after filtering\")\n   231→        io.close()\n   232→        return None\n   233→\n   234→    if sample_mode and len(valid_trial_indices) > 50:\n   235→        valid_trial_indices = valid_trial_indices[:50]\n   236→\n   237→    # ---- 6. Get brain region labels for good units ----\n   238→    brain_region_labels = []\n   239→    for i in good_indices:\n   240→        anno = units['anno_name'][i]\n   241→        if anno is None or anno == '' or anno == 'nan':\n   242→            anno = 'unknown'\n   243→        brain_region_labels.append(str(anno))\n   244→\n   245→    # ---- 7. Preload spike times for good units (for speed) ----\n   246→    print(f\"  Loading spike times for {len(good_indices)} good units...\")\n   247→    all_spike_times = preload_spike_times(nwb, good_indices)\n   248→    obs_intervals = units['obs_intervals'][good_indices[0]]\n   249→\n   250→    # ---- 8. Get tongue tracking data ----\n   251→    bts = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralTimeSeries']\n   252→    tongue_ts = bts.time_series['Camera0_side_TongueTracking']\n   253→    tongue_data = tongue_ts.data[:]  # (n_frames, 3): x, y, likelihood\n   254→    tongue_times = tongue_ts.timestamps[:]\n   255→    tongue_y = tongue_data[:, 1]\n   256→    tongue_likelihood = tongue_data[:, 2]\n   257→\n   258→    # ---- 9. Process each valid trial ----\n   259→    neural_list = []\n   260→    input_list = []\n   261→    output_list = []\n   262→    tongue_y_trial_values = []  # for computing session percentiles\n   263→\n   264→    for trial_idx in valid_trial_indices:\n   265→        go_cue = go_start_times[trial_idx]\n   266→        obs_idx = trial_to_obs[trial_idx]\n   267→\n   268→        # Get spike times for good units in this trial\n   269→        spike_times_trial = get_spike_times_for_trial_from_cache(all_spike_times, obs_intervals, obs_idx)\n   270→\n   271→        # Compute firing rates\n   272→        fr, bin_centers = compute_firing_rates(\n   273→            spike_times_trial, go_cue, BEGIN_TIME, END_TIME, BIN_WIDTH\n   274→        )\n   275→\n   276→        # ---- Inputs ----\n   277→        # 1. Time from tone onset (continuous, time-varying)\n   278→        # Find the last sample_start before this go cue (the actual sample epoch)\n   279→        t_start = trials['start_time'][trial_idx]\n   280→        valid_samples = sample_start_times[\n   281→            (sample_start_times >= t_start) & (sample_start_times < go_cue)\n   282→        ]\n   283→        if len(valid_samples) > 0:\n   284→            tone_onset = valid_samples[-1]  # last sample start = actual tone onset\n   285→        else:\n   286→            tone_onset = go_cue - 1.85  # fallback: typical sample-delay duration\n   287→\n   288→        tone_onset_rel = tone_onset - go_cue  # relative to go cue (should be ~-1.85)\n   289→        time_from_tone = bin_centers - tone_onset_rel  # time since tone onset at each bin\n   290→\n   291→        # 2. Whether photostimulation is on at every time point (binary)\n   292→        photostim_on = np.zeros(len(bin_centers), dtype=np.float32)\n   293→        photostim_onset_trial = trials['photostim_onset'][trial_idx]\n   294→        if photostim_onset_trial != 'N/A':\n   295→            onset_val = float(photostim_onset_trial)\n   296→            dur_val = float(trials['photostim_duration'][trial_idx])\n   297→            # photostim_onset is relative to trial start\n   298→            photostim_start_abs = t_start + onset_val\n   299→            photostim_stop_abs = photostim_start_abs + dur_val\n   300→            # Convert to relative to go cue\n   301→            ps_start_rel = photostim_start_abs - go_cue\n   302→            ps_stop_rel = photostim_stop_abs - go_cue\n   303→            photostim_on[(bin_centers >= ps_start_rel) & (bin_centers < ps_stop_rel)] = 1.0\n   304→\n   305→        input_trial = np.stack([time_from_tone.astype(np.float32), photostim_on], axis=0)  # (2, n_bins)\n   306→\n   307→        # ---- Outputs ----\n   308→        # 1. Choice: left=0, right=1\n   309→        instruction = trials['trial_instruction'][trial_idx]\n   310→        outcome = trials['outcome'][trial_idx]\n   311→        if outcome == 'hit':\n   312→            choice = 0 if instruction == 'left' else 1\n   313→        elif outcome == 'miss':\n   314→            choice = 1 if instruction == 'left' else 0  # miss means licked wrong side\n   315→        else:\n   316→            choice = 0  # shouldn't happen after filtering\n   317→\n   318→        # 2. Outcome: ignore=0, miss=1, hit=2\n   319→        outcome_map = {'ignore': 0, 'miss': 1, 'hit': 2}\n   320→        outcome_val = outcome_map.get(outcome, 0)\n   321→\n   322→        # 3. Early lick: no=0, yes=1 (should be all 0 after filtering)\n   323→        early_lick_val = 0 if trials['early_lick'][trial_idx] == 'no early' else 1\n   324→\n   325→        # 4. Tongue y-position (discretized per session) - get raw value first\n   326→        # Get tongue y values during this trial window using vectorized operations\n   327→        trial_window_start = go_cue + BEGIN_TIME - BIN_WIDTH\n   328→        trial_window_end = go_cue + END_TIME + BIN_WIDTH\n   329→        tw_mask = (tongue_times >= trial_window_start) & (tongue_times < trial_window_end)\n   330→        tw_times = tongue_times[tw_mask]\n   331→        tw_y = tongue_y[tw_mask]\n   332→        tw_lh = tongue_likelihood[tw_mask]\n   333→\n   334→        # Filter by likelihood > 0.5\n   335→        lh_mask = tw_lh > 0.5\n   336→        tw_times_good = tw_times[lh_mask]\n   337→        tw_y_good = tw_y[lh_mask]\n   338→\n   339→        tongue_y_bins = np.full(len(bin_centers), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   340→        if len(tw_times_good) > 0:\n   341→            # Use digitize for fast binning\n   342→            abs_bin_edges = np.linspace(BEGIN_TIME, END_TIME, len(bin_centers) + 1) + go_cue\n   343→            bin_assignments = np.digitize(tw_times_good, abs_bin_edges) - 1\n   344→            for b in range(len(bin_centers)):\n   345→                b_mask = bin_assignments == b\n   346→                if np.any(b_mask):\n   347→                    tongue_y_bins[b] = np.mean(tw_y_good[b_mask])\n   348→\n   349→        tongue_y_trial_values.append(tongue_y_bins)\n   350→\n   351→        output_trial = np.array([choice, outcome_val, early_lick_val], dtype=np.int64)  # (3,) - tongue added later\n   352→\n   353→        neural_list.append(fr)\n   354→        input_list.append(input_trial)\n   355→        output_list.append(output_trial)\n   356→\n   357→    io.close()\n   358→\n   359→    return {\n   360→        'neural': neural_list,\n   361→        'input': input_list,\n   362→        'output': output_list,\n   363→        'brain_region_labels': brain_region_labels,\n   364→        'subject_id': subject_id,\n   365→        'session_id': session_id,\n   366→        'tongue_y_trial_values': tongue_y_trial_values,\n   367→        'n_good_units': len(good_indices),\n   368→        'n_valid_trials': len(valid_trial_indices),\n   369→        'perf': perf,\n   370→    }\n   371→\n   372→\n   373→def map_region_to_major(anno_name):\n   374→    \"\"\"\n   375→    Map detailed CCF annotation to major brain region.\n   376→    Based on the reference code regions: ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum,\n   377→    Thalamus, Pons, Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus, OtherCortex, etc.\n   378→    \"\"\"\n   379→    anno = str(anno_name).strip().lower()\n   380→\n   381→    # ALM: Secondary motor area (MOs) and nearby\n   382→    if any(x in anno for x in ['secondary motor area', 'primary motor area', 'frontal pole']):\n   383→        return 'ALM'\n   384→\n   385→    # Striatum\n   386→    if any(x in anno for x in ['caudoputamen', 'striatum', 'nucleus accumbens', 'striatal']):\n   387→        return 'Striatum'\n   388→\n   389→    # Pallidum\n   390→    if any(x in anno for x in ['pallidum', 'globus pallidus', 'substantia innominata',\n   391→                                 'bed nuclei', 'diagonal band']):\n   392→        return 'Pallidum'\n   393→\n   394→    # Thalamus\n   395→    if any(x in anno for x in ['thalamus', 'geniculate', 'habenula',\n   396→                                 'zona incerta', 'reticular nucleus']):\n   397→        if 'hypothalamus' not in anno:\n   398→            return 'Thalamus'\n   399→\n   400→    # Hypothalamus\n   401→    if 'hypothalamus' in anno or 'hypothalamic' in anno:\n   402→        return 'Hypothalamus'\n   403→\n   404→    # Midbrain\n   405→    if any(x in anno for x in ['midbrain', 'superior colliculus', 'inferior colliculus',\n   406→                                 'substantia nigra', 'ventral tegmental',\n   407→                                 'periaqueductal', 'red nucleus', 'pretectal',\n   408→                                 'anterior pretectal', 'pedunculopontine']):\n   409→        return 'Midbrain'\n   410→\n   411→    # Pons\n   412→    if any(x in anno for x in ['pons', 'pontine', 'parabrachial', 'locus ceruleus',\n   413→                                 'barrington', 'tegmental nucleus', 'raphe']):\n   414→        return 'Pons'\n   415→\n   416→    # Medulla\n   417→    if any(x in anno for x in ['medulla', 'medullary', 'facial motor', 'hypoglossal',\n   418→                                 'vestibular', 'cochlear', 'spinal trigeminal',\n   419→                                 'gigantocellular', 'paragigantocellular',\n   420→                                 'lateral reticular', 'inferior olive',\n   421→                                 'dorsal column']):\n   422→        return 'Medulla'\n   423→\n   424→    # Cerebellum\n   425→    if any(x in anno for x in ['cerebellum', 'cerebellar', 'purkinje', 'lobul']):\n   426→        return 'Cerebellum'\n   427→\n   428→    # Hippocampus\n   429→    if any(x in anno for x in ['hippocampus', 'hippocampal', 'dentate', 'subiculum',\n   430→                                 'entorhinal', 'field ca', 'ammon']):\n   431→        return 'Hippocampus'\n   432→\n   433→    # Olfactory\n   434→    if any(x in anno for x in ['olfactory', 'piriform', 'olfact']):\n   435→        return 'Olfactory'\n   436→\n   437→    # Cortical subplate\n   438→    if any(x in anno for x in ['claustrum', 'endopiriform', 'cortical subplate',\n   439→                                 'basolateral amygdal', 'lateral amygdal',\n   440→                                 'basomedial amygdal', 'central amygdal',\n   441→                                 'medial amygdal', 'amygdal']):\n   442→        return 'CorticalSubplate'\n   443→\n   444→    # Orbital cortex\n   445→    if any(x in anno for x in ['orbital area', 'orbital cortex']):\n   446→        return 'Orbital'\n   447→\n   448→    # Other cortex (somatosensory, visual, auditory, insular, etc.)\n   449→    if any(x in anno for x in ['cortex', 'cortical', 'somatosensory', 'visual',\n   450→                                 'auditory', 'insular', 'agranular', 'retrosplenial',\n   451→                                 'cingulate', 'prelimbic', 'infralimbic',\n   452→                                 'anterior cingulate', 'layer', 'gustatory',\n   453→                                 'temporal association', 'perirhinal',\n   454→                                 'ectorhinal', 'visceral']):\n   455→        return 'OtherCortex'\n   456→\n   457→    # Fiber tracts\n   458→    if any(x in anno for x in ['fiber tract', 'corpus callosum', 'internal capsule',\n   459→                                 'cerebral peduncle', 'anterior commissure',\n   460→                                 'optic', 'tract']):\n   461→        return 'FiberTract'\n   462→\n   463→    if anno == 'unknown' or anno == '' or anno == 'nan':\n   464→        return 'unknown'\n   465→\n   466→    return 'OtherCortex'  # default\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_values, p40, p60):\n   470→    \"\"\"\n   471→    Discretize tongue y values:\n   472→    0: < 40th percentile\n   473→    1: 40th to 60th percentile\n   474→    2: > 60th percentile\n   475→    NaN values get class 0 (below 40th percentile - represents no tongue visible)\n   476→    \"\"\"\n   477→    result = np.zeros(len(tongue_y_values), dtype=np.int64)\n   478→    for i, val in enumerate(tongue_y_values):\n   479→        if np.isnan(val):\n   480→            result[i] = 0  # no tongue visible = low position\n   481→        elif val < p40:\n   482→            result[i] = 0\n   483→        elif val <= p60:\n   484→            result[i] = 1\n   485→        else:\n   486→            result[i] = 2\n   487→    return result\n   488→\n   489→\n   490→def convert_data(data_dir, sample_mode=False, max_sessions=None):\n   491→    \"\"\"Main conversion function.\"\"\"\n   492→\n   493→    subjects = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir) if d.startswith('sub-')])\n   494→\n   495→    all_sessions = []\n   496→    all_subject_ids = []\n   497→\n   498→    session_count = 0\n   499→\n   500→    for subj in subjects:\n   501→        subj_dir = os.path.join(data_dir, subj)\n   502→        files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(subj_dir) if f.endswith('.nwb')])\n   503→\n   504→        if sample_mode:\n   505→            files = files[:1]  # only first session per subject in sample mode\n   506→\n   507→        for fname in files:\n   508→            if max_sessions and session_count >= max_sessions:\n   509→                break\n   510→\n   511→            fpath = os.path.join(subj_dir, fname)\n   512→            print(f\"Processing {subj}/{fname}...\")\n   513→\n   514→            result = process_session(fpath, sample_mode=sample_mode)\n   515→            if result is not None:\n   516→                all_sessions.append(result)\n   517→                session_count += 1\n   518→\n   519→        if max_sessions and session_count >= max_sessions:\n   520→            break\n   521→\n   522→    if len(all_sessions) == 0:\n   523→        print(\"ERROR: No valid sessions found!\")\n   524→        return None\n   525→\n   526→    # ---- Build the data dictionary ----\n   527→\n   528→    # Subjects\n   529→    unique_subjects = list(OrderedDict.fromkeys(s['subject_id'] for s in all_sessions))\n   530→    subject_idx = np.array([unique_subjects.index(s['subject_id']) for s in all_sessions], dtype=np.int64)\n   531→\n   532→    # Brain regions\n   533→    all_region_labels = set()\n   534→    for s in all_sessions:\n   535→        for label in s['brain_region_labels']:\n   536→            all_region_labels.add(map_region_to_major(label))\n   537→\n   538→    # Remove 'unknown' and 'FiberTract' if present, sort for consistency\n   539→    brain_regions = sorted([r for r in all_region_labels if r not in ('unknown', 'FiberTract')])\n   540→    # Add unknown and FiberTract at the end if they exist\n   541→    if 'FiberTract' in all_region_labels:\n   542→        brain_regions.append('FiberTract')\n   543→    if 'unknown' in all_region_labels:\n   544→        brain_regions.append('unknown')\n   545→\n   546→    brain_region_idx = []\n   547→    for s in all_sessions:\n   548→        idx = np.array([brain_regions.index(map_region_to_major(label))\n   549→                        for label in s['brain_region_labels']], dtype=np.int64)\n   550→        brain_region_idx.append(idx)\n   551→\n   552→    # ---- Discretize tongue y per session ----\n   553→    neural_all = []\n   554→    input_all = []\n   555→    output_all = []\n   556→\n   557→    for s in all_sessions:\n   558→        # Compute session-level percentiles for tongue y\n   559→        all_tongue_y = np.concatenate([v for v in s['tongue_y_trial_values']])\n   560→        valid_tongue = all_tongue_y[~np.isnan(all_tongue_y)]\n   561→\n   562→        if len(valid_tongue) > 0:\n   563→            p40 = np.percentile(valid_tongue, 40)\n   564→            p60 = np.percentile(valid_tongue, 60)\n   565→        else:\n   566→            p40 = 0.0\n   567→            p60 = 0.0\n   568→\n   569→        neural_session = s['neural']\n   570→        input_session = s['input']\n   571→        output_session = []\n   572→\n   573→        for t_idx in range(len(s['output'])):\n   574→            base_output = s['output'][t_idx]  # (3,): choice, outcome, early_lick\n   575→            tongue_y_bins = s['tongue_y_trial_values'][t_idx]\n   576→            tongue_y_disc = discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_bins, p40, p60)\n   577→\n   578→            # Make tongue y a time-varying output: (1, n_bins)\n   579→            # Other outputs are per-trial: (3,)\n   580→            # We need to combine: make all outputs together\n   581→            # Per-trial outputs: choice, outcome, early_lick -> shape (n_output,)\n   582→            # Time-varying output: tongue_y -> shape (1, n_bins)\n   583→            # Since some are per-trial and one is time-varying, we make all time-varying\n   584→            n_bins = tongue_y_disc.shape[0]\n   585→            output_trial = np.zeros((4, n_bins), dtype=np.int64)\n   586→            output_trial[0, :] = base_output[0]  # choice (constant across time)\n   587→            output_trial[1, :] = base_output[1]  # outcome (constant across time)\n   588→            output_trial[2, :] = base_output[2]  # early_lick (constant across time)\n   589→            output_trial[3, :] = tongue_y_disc   # tongue y (time-varying)\n   590→\n   591→            output_session.append(output_trial)\n   592→\n   593→        neural_all.append(neural_session)\n   594→        input_all.append(input_session)\n   595→        output_all.append(output_session)\n   596→\n   597→    # ---- Build metadata ----\n   598→    n_bins = int(round((END_TIME - BEGIN_TIME) / BIN_WIDTH))\n   599→\n   600→    data = {\n   601→        'neural': neural_all,\n   602→        'input': input_all,\n   603→        'output': output_all,\n   604→\n   605→        'subjects': unique_subjects,\n   606→        'subject_idx': subject_idx,\n   607→\n   608→        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n   609→        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx,\n   610→\n   611→        'input_names': ['time_from_tone_onset', 'photostimulation'],\n   612→        'output_names': ['choice', 'outcome', 'early_lick', 'tongue_y_position'],\n   613→        'output_values': [\n   614→            ['left', 'right'],           # choice\n   615→            ['ignore', 'miss', 'hit'],   # outcome\n   616→            ['no', 'yes'],               # early_lick\n   617→            ['low', 'mid', 'high'],      # tongue_y_position\n   618→        ],\n   619→\n   620→        'metadata': {\n   621→            'task_description': 'Auditory delayed response task: mice hear tones during sample epoch, wait through delay, then lick left or right at go cue',\n   622→            'time_bin_size': BIN_WIDTH * 1000,  # in ms\n   623→            'temporal_alignment_event': 'Go cue onset',\n   624→            'off_start': BEGIN_TIME,\n   625→            'off_end': END_TIME,\n   626→            'n_bins': n_bins,\n   627→            'bin_width_s': BIN_WIDTH,\n   628→            'session_selection': f'Performance >{MIN_PERF*100}%, >= {MIN_CORRECT_PER_SIDE} correct per side',\n   629→            'trial_filtering': 'Excluded early lick and no-response (ignore) trials',\n   630→            'qc_method': 'Classifier-based QC (good classification in NWB)',\n   631→            'reference_paper': 'Li et al., Brain-wide analysis reveals movement encoding structured across and within brain areas',\n   632→        }\n   633→    }\n   634→\n   635→    return data\n   636→\n   637→\n   638→def print_stats(data):\n   639→    \"\"\"Print sanity check statistics.\"\"\"\n   640→    n_sessions = len(data['neural'])\n   641→    n_trials_total = sum(len(data['neural'][s]) for s in range(n_sessions))\n   642→    n_neurons_total = sum(data['neural'][s][0].shape[0] for s in range(n_sessions))\n   643→    n_subjects = len(data['subjects'])\n   644→\n   645→    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n   646→    print(f\"CONVERSION STATISTICS\")\n   647→    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n   648→    print(f\"Sessions: {n_sessions}\")\n   649→    print(f\"Subjects: {n_subjects}\")\n   650→    print(f\"Total trials: {n_trials_total}\")\n   651→    print(f\"Total neurons (sum across sessions): {n_neurons_total}\")\n   652→    print(f\"Trials per session: min={min(len(data['neural'][s]) for s in range(n_sessions))}, \"\n   653→          f\"max={max(len(data['neural'][s]) for s in range(n_sessions))}, \"\n   654→          f\"mean={n_trials_total/n_sessions:.1f}\")\n   655→\n   656→    neurons_per_session = [data['neural'][s][0].shape[0] for s in range(n_sessions)]\n   657→    print(f\"Neurons per session: min={min(neurons_per_session)}, max={max(neurons_per_session)}, \"\n   658→          f\"mean={np.mean(neurons_per_session):.1f}\")\n   659→\n   660→    n_bins = data['neural'][0][0].shape[1]\n   661→    print(f\"Time bins per trial: {n_bins}\")\n   662→    print(f\"Bin width: {data['metadata']['time_bin_size']} ms\")\n   663→    print(f\"Time window: {data['metadata']['off_start']} to {data['metadata']['off_end']} s\")\n   664→\n   665→    # Brain region distribution\n   666→    print(f\"\\nBrain region distribution:\")\n   667→    region_counts = {r: 0 for r in data['brain_regions']}\n   668→    for s in range(n_sessions):\n   669→        for idx in data['brain_region_idx'][s]:\n   670→            region_counts[data['brain_regions'][idx]] += 1\n   671→    for region, count in sorted(region_counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):\n   672→        print(f\"  {region}: {count}\")\n   673→\n   674→    # Sessions per subject\n   675→    print(f\"\\nSessions per subject:\")\n   676→    from collections import Counter\n   677→    subj_counts = Counter(data['subject_idx'])\n   678→    for subj_idx, count in sorted(subj_counts.items()):\n   679→        print(f\"  {data['subjects'][subj_idx]}: {count}\")\n   680→\n   681→    # Output distribution\n   682→    print(f\"\\nOutput distributions:\")\n   683→    for out_dim in range(len(data['output_names'])):\n   684→        all_vals = []\n   685→        for s in range(n_sessions):\n   686→            for t in range(len(data['output'][s])):\n   687→                out = data['output'][s][t]\n   688→                if out.ndim == 1:\n   689→                    all_vals.append(out[out_dim])\n   690→                else:\n   691→                    all_vals.extend(out[out_dim, :].tolist())\n   692→        vals, counts = np.unique(all_vals, return_counts=True)\n   693→        total = sum(counts)\n   694→        print(f\"  {data['output_names'][out_dim]}:\")\n   695→        for v, c in zip(vals, counts):\n   696→            name = data['output_values'][out_dim][int(v)] if int(v) < len(data['output_values'][out_dim]) else f'val{int(v)}'\n   697→            print(f\"    {name} ({int(v)}): {c} ({c/total*100:.1f}%)\")\n   698→\n   699→\n   700→if __name__ == '__main__':\n   701→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n   702→    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Create a small sample dataset')\n   703→    parser.add_argument('--max-sessions', type=int, default=None, help='Maximum number of sessions to process')\n   704→    parser.add_argument('--output', type=str, default=None, help='Output pickle file path')\n   705→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   706→\n   707→    if args.sample:\n   708→        print(\"Creating sample dataset...\")\n   709→        output_file = args.output or '/app/sample_data.pkl'\n   710→        data = convert_data(DATA_DIR, sample_mode=True, max_sessions=5)\n   711→    else:\n   712→        print(\"Creating full dataset...\")\n   713→        output_file = args.output or '/app/converted_data.pkl'\n   714→        data = convert_data(DATA_DIR, sample_mode=False, max_sessions=args.max_sessions)\n   715→\n   716→    if data is not None:\n   717→        print_stats(data)\n   718→\n   719→        print(f\"\\nSaving to {output_file}...\")\n   720→        with 50 ms bins\nBEGIN_TIME = -2.5  # relative to go cue\nEND_TIME = 1.5    # relative to go cue\nMIN_PERF = 0.65   # >65% correct\nMIN_CORRECT_PER_SIDE = 50  # at least 50 correct per side\n\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\n\ndef compute_firing_rates(spike_times_by_neuron, go_cue_time, begin_time, end_time, bin_width):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute firing rates for a single trial.\n\n    Args:\n        spike_times_by_neuron: list of arrays, spike times for each neuron (absolute times)\n        go_cue_time: float, absolute go cue time for this trial\n        begin_time: float, start of window relative to go cue\n        end_time: float, end of window relative to go cue\n        bin_width: float, bin width in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        fr: (n_neurons, n_bins) array of firing rates\n        bin_centers: (n_bins,) array of bin centers relative to go cue\n    \"\"\"\n    n_bins = int(round((end_time - begin_time) / bin_width))\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(begin_time, end_time, n_bins + 1) + go_cue_time\n    bin_centers = (bin_edges[:-1] + bin_edges[1:]) / 2.0 - go_cue_time\n\n    n_neurons = len(spike_times_by_neuron)\n    fr = np.zeros((n_neurons, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for i, st in enumerate(spike_times_by_neuron):\n        if len(st) == 0:\n            continue\n        # Only consider spikes in the window\n        mask = (st >= bin_edges[0]) & (st < bin_edges[-1])\n        st_window = st[mask]\n        if len(st_window) > 0:\n            counts, _ = np.histogram(st_window, bins=bin_edges)\n            fr[i, :] = counts.astype(np.float32) / bin_width\n\n    return fr, bin_centers\n\n\ndef preload_spike_times(nwb, unit_indices):\n    \"\"\"\n    Preload all spike times for good units into memory.\n    Returns list of arrays, one per unit.\n    \"\"\"\n    return [nwb.units['spike_times'][uid] for uid in unit_indices]\n\n\ndef get_spike_times_for_trial_from_cache(all_spike_times, obs_intervals, obs_idx):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get spike times for a specific obs_interval index using preloaded data.\n    \"\"\"\n    spike_times_list = []\n    t_start, t_stop = obs_intervals[obs_idx]\n    for st in all_spike_times:\n        mask = (st >= t_start) & (st < t_stop)\n        spike_times_list.append(st[mask])\n    return spike_times_list\n\n\ndef build_obs_to_trial_map(nwb, good_indices):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build mapping from obs_intervals indices to trial table indices.\n    Returns:\n        obs_to_trial: list of trial indices, where obs_to_trial[obs_idx] = trial_idx\n    \"\"\"\n    trials = nwb.trials\n    n_trials = len(trials)\n    units = nwb.units\n\n    # Use the first good unit's obs_intervals (all units have the same)\n    obs = units['obs_intervals'][good_indices[0]]\n    n_obs = obs.shape[0]\n\n    if n_obs == n_trials:\n        # Perfect match - obs[i] corresponds to trial[i]\n        return list(range(n_trials))\n\n    # Match by start time\n    trial_starts = np.array([trials['start_time'][i] for i in range(n_trials)])\n    obs_to_trial = []\n    for i in range(n_obs):\n        diffs = np.abs(trial_starts - obs[i, 0])\n        min_idx = np.argmin(diffs)\n        if diffs[min_idx] < 0.5:  # 0.5s tolerance\n            obs_to_trial.append(int(min_idx))\n        else:\n            obs_to_trial.append(-1)  # no match\n\n    return obs_to_trial\n\n\ndef process_session(nwb_path, sample_mode=False):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process one NWB session file.\n\n    Returns None if session doesn't pass criteria, otherwise returns a dict with:\n        - neural: list of (n_neurons, n_bins) arrays per trial\n        - input: list of (n_input, n_bins) arrays per trial\n        - output: list of (n_output,) arrays per trial\n        - brain_region_labels: list of str per neuron\n        - subject_id: str\n        - session_id: str\n        - tongue_y_all: list of tongue_y values for percentile computation\n    \"\"\"\n    io = pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_path, 'r')\n    nwb = io.read()\n\n    trials = nwb.trials\n    n_trials = len(trials)\n    units = nwb.units\n    n_units = len(units)\n\n    subject_id = nwb.subject.description  # Find good units ----\n    good_indices = []\n    for i in range(n_units):\n        if units['classification'][i] == 'good':\n            good_indices.append(i)\n\n    if len(good_indices) == 0:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}: 0 good units\")\n        io.close()\n        return None\n\n    # ---- 2. Build obs_intervals to trial mapping ----\n    obs_to_trial = build_obs_to_trial_map(nwb, good_indices)\n    # Set of trial indices that have neural data\n    trials_with_neural = set(t for t in obs_to_trial if t >= 0)\n    # Build reverse mapping: trial_idx -> obs_idx\n    trial_to_obs = {}\n    for obs_idx, trial_idx in enumerate(obs_to_trial):\n        if trial_idx >= 0:\n            trial_to_obs[trial_idx] = obs_idx\n\n    # ---- 3. Get behavioral events ----\n    be = nwb.acquisition['BehavioralEvents']\n    go_start_times = be.time_series['go_start_times'].timestamps[:]\n    sample_start_times = be.time_series['sample_start_times'].timestamps[:]\n\n    # ---- 4. Check session selection criteria ----\n    # Compute performance on control (no photostim) non-early-lick trials\n    # Use ALL trials for session selection (not just those with neural data)\n    control_no_early = 0\n    correct_control = 0\n    correct_left = 0\n    correct_right = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        is_control = trials['photostim_onset'][i] == 'N/A'\n        is_no_early = trials['early_lick'][i] == 'no early'\n        is_hit = trials['outcome'][i] == 'hit'\n        instruction = trials['trial_instruction'][i]\n\n        if is_control and is_no_early:\n            control_no_early += 1\n            if is_hit:\n                correct_control += 1\n                if instruction == 'left':\n                    correct_left += 1\n                else:\n                    correct_right += 1\n\n    if control_no_early == 0:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}: no control non-early trials\")\n        io.close()\n        return None\n\n    perf = correct_control / control_no_early\n    if perf <= MIN_PERF or correct_left < MIN_CORRECT_PER_SIDE or correct_right < MIN_CORRECT_PER_SIDE:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}: perf={perf:.1%}, L={correct_left}, R={correct_right}\")\n        io.close()\n        return None\n\n    # ---- 5. Filter trials ----\n    # Per methods: \"Early lick trials and no response trials were excluded for analysis\"\n    # Also must have neural data (be in obs_intervals)\n    valid_trial_indices = []\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        # Must have neural data\n        if i not in trials_with_neural:\n            continue\n\n        outcome = trials['outcome'][i]\n        early = trials['early_lick'][i]\n\n        # Exclude early lick and ignore (no response) trials\n        if early != 'no early':\n            continue\n        if outcome == 'ignore':\n            continue\n\n        valid_trial_indices.append(i)\n\n    if len(valid_trial_indices) < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {os.path.basename(nwb_path)}: <2 valid trials after filtering\")\n        io.close()\n        return None\n\n    if sample_mode and len(valid_trial_indices) > 50:\n        valid_trial_indices = valid_trial_indices[:50]\n\n    # ---- 6. Get brain region labels for good units ----\n    brain_region_labels = []\n    for i in good_indices:\n        anno = units['anno_name'][i]\n        if anno is None or anno == '' or anno == 'nan':\n            anno = 'unknown'\n        brain_region_labels.append(str(anno))\n\n    # ---- 7. Preload spike times for good units (for speed) ----\n    print(f\"  Loading spike times for {len(good_indices)} good units...\")\n    all_spike_times = preload_spike_times(nwb, good_indices)\n    obs_intervals = units['obs_intervals'][good_indices[0]]\n\n    # ---- 8. Process each valid trial ----\n    neural_list = []\n    input_list = []\n    output_list = []\n    tongue_y_trial_values = []  # for computing session percentiles\n\n    for trial_idx in valid_trial_indices:\n        go_cue = go_start_times[trial_idx]\n        obs_idx = trial_to_obs[trial_idx]\n\n        # Get spike times for good units in this trial\n        spike_times_trial = get_spike_times_for_trial_from_cache(all_spike_times, obs_intervals, obs_idx)\n\n        # Compute firing rates\n        fr, bin_centers = compute_firing_rates(\n            spike_times_trial, go_cue, BEGIN_TIME, END_TIME, BIN_WIDTH\n        )\n\n        # ---- Inputs ----\n        # 1. Time from tone onset (continuous, time-varying)\n        # Find the last sample_start before this go cue (the actual sample epoch)\n        t_start = trials['start_time'][trial_idx]\n        valid_samples = sample_start_times[\n            (sample_start_times >= t_start) & (sample_start_times < go_cue)\n        ]\n        if len(valid_samples) > 0:\n            tone_onset = valid_samples[-1]  # last sample start = actual tone onset\n        else:\n            tone_onset = go_cue - 1.85  # fallback: typical sample-delay duration\n\n        tone_onset_rel = tone_onset - go_cue  # relative to go cue (should be ~-1.85)\n        time_from_tone = bin_centers - tone_onset_rel  # time since tone onset at each bin\n\n        # 2. Whether photostimulation is on at every time point (binary)\n        photostim_on = np.zeros(len(bin_centers), dtype=np.float32)\n        photostim_onset_trial = trials['photostim_onset'][trial_idx]\n        if photostim_onset_trial != 'N/A':\n            onset_val = float(photostim_onset_trial)\n            dur_val = float(trials['photostim_duration'][trial_idx])\n            # photostim_onset is relative to trial start\n            photostim_start_abs = t_start + onset_val\n            photostim_stop_abs = photostim_start_abs + dur_val\n            # Convert to relative to go cue\n            ps_start_rel = photostim_start_abs - go_cue\n            ps_stop_rel = photostim_stop_abs - go_cue\n            photostim_on[(bin_centers >= ps_start_rel) & (bin_centers < ps_stop_rel)] = 1.0\n\n        input_trial = np.stack([time_from_tone.astype(np.float32), photostim_on], axis=0)  # (2, n_bins)\n\n        # ---- Outputs ----\n        # 1. Choice: left=0, right=1\n        instruction = trials['trial_instruction'][trial_idx]\n        outcome = trials['outcome'][trial_idx]\n        if outcome == 'hit':\n            choice = 0 if instruction == 'left' else 1\n        elif outcome == 'miss':\n            choice = 1 if instruction == 'left' else 0  # miss means licked wrong side\n        else:\n            choice = 0  # shouldn't happen after filtering\n\n        # 2. Outcome: ignore=0, miss=1, hit=2\n        outcome_map = {'ignore': 0, 'miss': 1, 'hit': 2}\n        outcome_val = outcome_map.get(outcome, 0)\n\n        # 3. Early lick: no=0, yes=1 (should be all 0 after filtering)\n        early_lick_val = 0 if trials['early_lick'][trial_idx] == 'no early' else 1\n\n        # 4. Tongue y-position (discretized per session) - get raw value first\n        # Get tongue y values during this trial window using vectorized operations\n        trial_window_start = go_cue + BEGIN_TIME - BIN_WIDTH\n        trial_window_end = go_cue + END_TIME + BIN_WIDTH\n        tw_mask = (tongue_times >= trial_window_start) & (tongue_times < trial_window_end)\n        tw_times = tongue_times[tw_mask]\n        tw_y = tongue_y[tw_mask]\n        tw_lh = tongue_likelihood[tw_mask]\n\n        # Filter by likelihood > 0.5\n        lh_mask = tw_lh > 0.5\n        tw_times_good = tw_times[lh_mask]\n        tw_y_good = tw_y[lh_mask]\n\n        tongue_y_bins = np.full(len(bin_centers), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n        if len(tw_times_good) > 0:\n            # Use digitize for fast binning\n            abs_bin_edges = np.linspace(BEGIN_TIME, END_TIME, len(bin_centers) + 1) + go_cue\n            bin_assignments = np.digitize(tw_times_good, abs_bin_edges) - 1\n            for b in range(len(bin_centers)):\n                b_mask = bin_assignments == b\n                if np.any(b_mask):\n                    tongue_y_bins[b] = np.mean(tw_y_good[b_mask])\n\n        tongue_y_trial_values.append(tongue_y_bins)\n\n        output_trial = np.array([choice, outcome_val, early_lick_val], dtype=np.int64)  # (3,) - tongue added later\n\n        neural_list.append(fr)\n        input_list.append(input_trial)\n        output_list.append(output_trial)\n\n    io.close()\n\n    return {\n        'neural': neural_list,\n        'input': input_list,\n        'output': output_list,\n        'brain_region_labels': brain_region_labels,\n        'subject_id': subject_id,\n        'session_id': session_id,\n        'tongue_y_trial_values': tongue_y_trial_values,\n        'n_good_units': len(good_indices),\n        'n_valid_trials': len(valid_trial_indices),\n        'perf': perf,\n    }\n\n\ndef map_region_to_major(anno_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Map detailed CCF annotation to major brain region.\n    Based on the reference code regions: ALM, Medulla, Midbrain, Striatum,\n    Thalamus, Pons, Cerebellum, Hypothalamus, Hippocampus, OtherCortex, etc.\n    \"\"\"\n    anno = str(anno_name).strip().lower()\n\n    # ALM: Secondary motor area (MOs) and nearby\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['secondary motor area', 'primary motor area', 'frontal pole']):\n        return 'ALM'\n\n    # Striatum\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['caudoputamen', 'striatum', 'nucleus accumbens', 'striatal']):\n        return 'Striatum'\n\n    # Pallidum\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['pallidum', 'globus pallidus', 'substantia innominata',\n                                 'bed nuclei', 'diagonal band']):\n        return 'Pallidum'\n\n    # Thalamus\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['thalamus', 'geniculate', 'habenula',\n                                 'zona incerta', 'reticular nucleus']):\n        if 'hypothalamus' not in anno:\n            return 'Thalamus'\n\n    # Hypothalamus\n    if 'hypothalamus' in anno or 'hypothalamic' in anno:\n        return 'Hypothalamus'\n\n    # Midbrain\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['midbrain', 'superior colliculus', 'inferior colliculus',\n                                 'substantia nigra', 'ventral tegmental',\n                                 'periaqueductal', 'red nucleus', 'pretectal',\n                                 'anterior pretectal', 'pedunculopontine']):\n        return 'Midbrain'\n\n    # Pons\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['pons', 'pontine', 'parabrachial', 'locus ceruleus',\n                                 'barrington', 'tegmental nucleus', 'raphe']):\n        return 'Pons'\n\n    # Medulla\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['medulla', 'medullary', 'facial motor', 'hypoglossal',\n                                 'vestibular', 'cochlear', 'spinal trigeminal',\n                                 'gigantocellular', 'paragigantocellular',\n                                 'lateral reticular', 'inferior olive',\n                                 'dorsal column']):\n        return 'Medulla'\n\n    # Cerebellum\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['cerebellum', 'cerebellar', 'purkinje', 'lobul']):\n        return 'Cerebellum'\n\n    # Hippocampus\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['hippocampus', 'hippocampal', 'dentate', 'subiculum',\n                                 'entorhinal', 'field ca', 'ammon']):\n        return 'Hippocampus'\n\n    # Olfactory\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['olfactory', 'piriform', 'olfact']):\n        return 'Olfactory'\n\n    # Cortical subplate\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['claustrum', 'endopiriform', 'cortical subplate',\n                                 'basolateral amygdal', 'lateral amygdal',\n                                 'basomedial amygdal', 'central amygdal',\n                                 'medial amygdal', 'amygdal']):\n        return 'CorticalSubplate'\n\n    # Orbital cortex\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['orbital area', 'orbital cortex']):\n        return 'Orbital'\n\n    # Other cortex (somatosensory, visual, auditory, insular, etc.)\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['cortex', 'cortical', 'somatosensory', 'visual',\n                                 'auditory', 'insular', 'agranular', 'retrosplenial',\n                                 'cingulate', 'prelimbic', 'infralimbic',\n                                 'anterior 'OtherCortex'\n\n    # Fiber tracts\n    if any(x in anno for x in ['fiber tract', 'corpus callosum', 'internal capsule',\n                                 'cerebral peduncle', 'anterior commissure',\n                                 'optic', 'tract']):\n        return 'FiberTract'\n\n    if anno == 'unknown' or anno == '' or anno == 'nan':\n        return 'unknown'\n\n    return 'OtherCortex'  # default\n\n\ndef discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_values, p40, p60):\n    \"\"\"\n    Discretize tongue y values:\n    0: < 40th percentile\n    1: 40th to 60th percentile\n    2: > 60th percentile\n    NaN values get class 0 (below 40th percentile - represents no tongue visible)\n    \"\"\"\n    result = np.zeros(len(tongue_y_values), dtype=np.int64)\n    for i, val in enumerate(tongue_y_values):\n        if np.isnan(val):\n            result[i] = 0  # no tongue visible = low position\n        elif val < p40:\n            result[i] = 0\n        elif val <= p60:\n            result[i] = 1\n        else:\n            result[i] = 2\n    return result\n\n\ndef convert_data(data_dir, sample_mode=False, max_sessions=None):\n    \"\"\"Main conversion function.\"\"\"\n\n    subjects = sorted([d for d in os.listdir(data_dir) if d.startswith('sub-')])\n\n    all_sessions = []\n    all_subject_ids = []\n\n    session_count = 0\n\n    for subj in subjects:\n        subj_dir = os.path.join(data_dir, subj)\n        files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(subj_dir) if f.endswith('.nwb')])\n\n        if sample_mode:\n            files = files[:1]  # only first session per subject in sample mode\n\n        for fname in files:\n            if max_sessions and session_count >= max_sessions:\n                break\n\n            fpath = os.path.join(subj_dir, fname)\n            print(f\"Processing {subj}/{fname}...\")\n\n            result = process_session(fpath, sample_mode=sample_mode)\n            if result is not None:\n                all_sessions.append(result)\n                session_count += 1\n\n        if max_sessions and session_count >= max_sessions:\n            break\n\n    if len(all_sessions) == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No valid sessions found!\")\n        return None\n\n    # ---- Build the data dictionary ----\n\n    # Subjects\n    unique_subjects = list(OrderedDict.fromkeys(s['subject_id'] for s in all_sessions))\n    subject_idx = np.array([unique_subjects.index(s['subject_id']) for s in all_sessions], dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # Brain regions\n    all_region_labels = set()\n    for s in all_sessions:\n        for label in s['brain_region_labels']:\n            all_region_labels.add(map_region_to_major(label))\n\n    # Remove 'unknown' and 'FiberTract' if present, sort for consistency\n    brain_regions = sorted([r for r in all_region_labels if r not in ('unknown', 'FiberTract')])\n    # Add unknown and FiberTract at the end if they exist\n    if 'FiberTract' in all_region_labels:\n        brain_regions.append('FiberTract')\n    if 'unknown' in all_region_labels:\n        brain_regions.append('unknown')\n\n    brain_region_idx = []\n    for s in all_sessions:\n        idx = np.array([brain_regions.index(map_region_to_major(label))\n                        for label in s['brain_region_labels']], dtype=np.int64)\n        brain_region_idx.append(idx)\n\n    # ---- Discretize tongue y per session ----\n    neural_all = []\n    input_all = []\n    output_all = []\n\n    for s in all_sessions:\n        # Compute session-level percentiles for tongue y\n        all_tongue_y = np.concatenate([v for v in s['tongue_y_trial_values']])\n        valid_tongue = all_tongue_y[~np.isnan(all_tongue_y)]\n\n        if len(valid_tongue) > 0:\n            p40 = np.percentile(valid_tongue, 40)\n            p60 = np.percentile(valid_tongue, 60)\n        else:\n            p40 = 0.0\n            p60 = 0.0\n\n        neural_session = s['neural']\n        input_session = s['input']\n        output_session = []\n\n        for t_idx in range(len(s['output'])):\n            base_output = s['output'][t_idx]  # (3,): choice, outcome, early_lick\n            tongue_y_bins = s['tongue_y_trial_values'][t_idx]\n            tongue_y_disc = discretize_tongue_y(tongue_y_bins, p40, p60)\n\n            # Make tongue y a time-varying output: (1, n_bins)\n            # Other outputs are per-trial: (3,)\n            # We need to combine: make all outputs together\n            # Per-trial outputs: choice, outcome, early_lick -> shape (n_output,)\n            # Time-varying output: tongue_y -> shape (1, n_bins)\n            # Since some are per-trial and one is time-varying, we make all time-varying\n            n_bins = tongue_y_disc.shape[0]\n            output_trial = np.zeros((4, n_bins), dtype=np.int64)\n            output_trial[0, :] = base_output[0]  # choice (constant across time)\n            output_trial[1, :] = base_output[1]  # outcome (constant across time)\n            output_trial[2, :] = base_output[2]  # early_lick (constant across time)\n            output_trial[3, :] = tongue_y_disc   # tongue y (time-varying)\n\n            output_session.append(output_trial)\n\n        neural_all.append(neural_session)\n        input_all.append(input_session)\n        output_all.append(output_session)\n\n    # ---- Build metadata ----\n    n_bins = int(round((END_TIME - BEGIN_TIME) / BIN_WIDTH))\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': neural_all,\n        'input': Conversion Notes: MAP Dataset NWB to Decoder Format\n\n## Source Data\n- **Dataset**: MAP (Mesoscale Activity Project), Li et al.\n- **Format**: NWB (Neurodata Without Borders) files\n- **Location**: `/app/data/` (28 subjects, 174 sessions)\n- **Task**: Auditory delayed response task with Neuropixels recordings\n\n## Conversion Parameters\n| Parameter | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| Bin width lick-right correct trials |\n| QC method | Classifier-based ('good' classification in NWB) |\n| Trial filtering | Exclude early lick and no-response (ignore) trials |\n\n## Output Summary\n| Metric | Temporal Alignment\nAll neural data is aligned to go cue onset (time 0). The time window spans -2.5s to +1.5s, covering:\n- Pre-sample baseline\n- Sample epoch (tone presentation, ~1.85s before go cue)\n- Delay epoch\n- Go cue and response\n\n### 2. Firing Rate Computation\nSpike counts are histogrammed into 50ms bins and divided by bin width (0.05s) to get firing rates in Hz. This is simpler than the sliding kernel approach in the original preprocessing code but appropriate for the decoder task.\n\n### 3. obs_intervals Handling\nSome NWB files have units with `obs_intervals` that cover only a subset of trials (e.g., 160 obs_intervals for 480 trials). This occurs when recording was started/stopped within a session. The conversion builds a mapping from obs_intervals indices to trial table indices based on temporal overlap between observation windows and trial events. Only trials with neural data coverage are included.\n\n### 4. Trial Filtering\nFollowing the paper's methodology:\n- **Early lick trials**: Excluded (animals licked before go cue)\n- **No-response (ignore) trials**: Excluded (no lick within response window)\n- After filtering, `early_lick` output is always 0 and `outcome` never has value 0 (ignore). The output_values definitions still list all possible values for completeness.\n\n### 5. Session Selection Criteria\nApplied to control (non-photostimulation) trials only:\n- Performance must exceed 65%\n- At least 50 correct lick-left and 50 correct lick-right trials required\n- 69 sessions were excluded (skipped); 105 sessions passed\n\n### 6. Choice Determination\n- **Hit trials**: choice = instruction side (the instructed lick direction)\n- **Miss trials**: choice = opposite of instruction side (animal chose wrong side)\n\n### 7. Tone Onset (time_from_tone_onset)\nThe tone onset time is identified as the last `sample_start_time` before the go cue. For most sessions, this produces `time_from_tone_onset` values ranging from ~-0.6 to ~3.3 (relative to each bin center).\n\nNotable exceptions:\n- **Session 13**: One trial has unusual tone onset (~7.4s range), likely from a replay/repeated stimulus\n- **Sessions 40-104** (subject SC038 and later): Range is [-1.5, 3.9], indicating a different sample-to-go-cue timing (~1.85s vs ~0.65s earlier sessions). This reflects genuine experimental variation across subjects.\n\n### 8. Photostimulation\nBinary input (0=off, 1=on). Photostim onset times from NWB are converted from absolute times to relative-to-go-cue times. If any photostim occurs within the trial window [-2.5, 1.5], photostimulation is set to 1.\n\n### 9. Brain Region Mapping\nDetailed CCF (Common Coordinate Framework) annotations are mapped to 15 major regions:\nALM, Striatum, Thalamus, Orbital, OtherCortex, Midbrain, Olfactory, Cerebellum, Hippocampus, CorticalSubplate, Pallidum, Medulla, Pons, Hypothalamus, FiberTract.\n\n### 10. Tongue Y-Position Discretization\n- Extracted from DeepLabCut tracking (side camera, Camera0)\n- Only positions with tracking likelihood > 0.5 are used\n- Per-session discretization using 40th and 60th percentiles:\n  - 0 (low): below 40th percentile\n  - 1 (mid): between 40th and 60th percentiles\n  - 2 (high): above 60th percentile\n- Time bins without valid tracking data default to 0 (low)\n\n## Known Issues / Warnings\n\n### All-Zero Neural Data\nSome trials in sessions 40-42 (and a few others) have all-zero neural data across all neurons and time bins. This occurs because the obs_intervals for those trials overlap very little with the actual firing window, resulting in no spikes being captured. These trials are retained in the data but flagged as warnings during verification.\n\n## Decoder Training Results\n\n### Full Dataset (105 sessions, 48,356 trials)\n| Output | Balanced Accuracy | Chance |\n|--------|------------------|--------|\n| Choice | 72.4% | 50.0% |\n| Outcome | 67.9% | 33.3% |\n| Early lick | 100% (trivial) | 50.0% |\n| Tongue y-position | 71.5% | 33.3% |\n\n### Sample Dataset (5 sessions, 250 trials)\n| Output | Balanced Accuracy | Chance |\n|--------|------------------|--------|\n| Choice | 67.5% | 50.0% |\n| Outcome | 64.5% | 33.3% |\n| Early lick | 100% (trivial) | 50.0% |\n| Tongue y-position | 56.9% | 33.3% |\n","numLines":105,"startLine":1,"totalLines":105}}}
